# Graph Report - deepseek-harness  (2026-08-26)

## Corpus Check
- 6154 files · ~4,784,724 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 64041 nodes · 112650 edges · 4257 communities (3613 shown, 644 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1172 edges (avg confidence: 0.83)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `141eb6fe`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
